### direct-ja


### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para actualizar tu rama local o privada con los cambios de la rama principal y limpiar tus commits antes de compartirlos. Usa **merge** cuando la rama ya está compartida o publicada, porque reescribir su historial obligaría a los demás a arreglar sus copias.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 可靠、有连接，保证数据不丢、不乱序；UDP 无连接、不保证送达，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local branch onto the latest main before it's shared, so its history reads as one clean line. Merge when the branch is already pushed or shared, since rewriting published history breaks everyone else's copies.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径作为参数就行：`bun test tests/foo.test.ts`。它也会把参数当作文件名的子串匹配，所以 `bun test foo` 会跑所有路径里含 `foo` 的测试文件。要按测试名筛选，用 `bun test -t "测试名"`。
```

Keep the doc short: a title, a one-paragraph summary, one table of the key load-test numbers, and a few lines on the setup (dataset size, concurrency, duration, machine if the bench files say so). ⏎  ⏎ Done criteria: a markdown doc written in ~/dev/hawdb/bench (name it LOAD_TEST_REPORT.md) that reports only numbers actually present in the bench files or their result output. Every number must trace to a file in that folder. Report the path of the doc and the source files you used. If a result is only in a unit that is not metric (for example MiB or lb), convert it and say you converted it. If numbers are missing, incomplete or inconsistent across files, say so in the doc and in your report instead of guessing. ⏎  ⏎ Out of scope: do not change the bench code, do not re-run the load tests, do not edit any existing result files, do not push, open a PR or merge anything. Do not invent nu

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data files. After a crash, the database replays that log on restart to recover committed changes that hadn't yet reached the data files.
```

```
用户原话：“接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点”

要做的：
- 找到 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把 compaction 调度器里过粗的锁拆细，只保护真正需要互斥的数据。
- 保持现有行为不变；跑相关测试确认没有回归，并在报告里贴出测试输出。
- 改动提交在当前分支 ducter/compaction-scheduler 上。

不做的事：不合并，不推送，不打开或修改 PR 的状态，不改 PR 的 review 说明。

完成标准：锁粒度改动已提交，测试通过，报告里说明改了哪几处锁、各自保护什么。
  [also used tools] task_find
```

```
(function(){

    const amount = parseFloat($('fAmt').value);
    if (!day) { $('fDay').focus(); return; }

    if (editId) {
      items = items.map(i => i.id === editId ? { ...i, day, item, amount } : i);
      fillForm(null);
    } else {
      items.push({ id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), day, item, amount });
      fillForm(null, true);
      $('fItem').value = '';
      $('fAmt').value = '';
    }
    persist();
  };

  $('cancel').onclick = () => fillForm(null);
  $('del').onclick = () => {
    items = items.filter(i => i.id !== editId);
    fillForm(null);
    persist();
  };

  $('days').onclick = e => {
    const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    const it = items.find(i => i.id === row.dataset.id);
    if (!it) return;
    fillForm(it);
    $('addBox').open = true;
    $('fItem').focus();
  };

  function load(v) {
    items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  }
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load);
  ducter.onChange(() => Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load));
})();
```

```
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>

  <div class="k-field"><label>什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal"></div>

</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  const t=document.getElementById("total");
  t.innerHTML=xs.length?fmt(total):'<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"北京出差";
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  if(!xs.length){const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="还没有花销，记上第一笔";list.appendChild(e);return;}
  for(const x of xs){
    const r=document.createElement("div");r.className="k-row";

    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    r.appendChild(m);r.appendChild(s);r.appendChild(v);list.appendChild(r);
  }
}
document.getElementById("add").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  if(!what||isNaN(yuan))return;
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
  document.getElementById("day").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}
```

```
function dayKey(d){ return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }
function nowHM(d){ return pad(d.getHours()) + ':' + pad(d.getMinutes()); }
var today = dayKey(new Date());

function load(){
  var v = ducter.get('feeds');
  feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];

}

}

  if (!row) return;

});

});

document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function(){

  var t = tv || nowHM(new Date());
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t)) return;
  feeds.push({ d: today, t: t, ml: mlv });
  ducter.set('feeds', feeds);

  document.getElementById('t').value = '';
  render();
});

ducter.onChange(load);
load();
```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs and a tick box on each. Tick one when it lands and the count drops.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1728 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one as it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
const PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
let landed = {};
function esc(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c])); }
function render() {
  const list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = PRS.map(p => {
    const on = !!landed[p.id];
    return '<label class="k-row" style="cursor:pointer">' +
      '<input type="checkbox" data-id="' + p.id + '"' + (on ? ' checked' : '') + '>' +
      '<div class="k-row-main" style="' + (on ? 'text-decoration:line-through;opacity:.5' : '') + '">' +
        '<div>' + esc(p.title) + ' · ' + esc(p.what) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(p.status) + '</div>' +
      '</div></label>';
  }).join('');
  const left = PRS.filter(p => !landed[p.id]).length;
  document.getElementById('left').textContent = left;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = left === 0 ? 'All landed' : 'Tick each one as it lands';
  list.querySelectorAll('input[type=checkbox]').forEach(cb => {
    cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
      landed[cb.dataset.id] = cb.checked;
      ducter.set('landed', landed);
      ducter.summary(left + ' still waiting');
      render();
    });
  });
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => { landed = v || {}; render(); });
ducter.onChange(() => Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => { landed = v || {}; render(); }));
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412","what":"Compaction cut","status":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","what":"Retry on 502","status":"CI failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","status":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418","what":"Apps bar","status":"Draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.

--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻

  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话（回复你上一条）：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

决定：在现有的 TTL 假时钟测试基础上，再加一条测试，覆盖缓存项恰好在 TTL 到期时刻（时钟正好走到过期时间点）的行为。

完成标准：
- 新测试用假时钟，把时间推进到恰好等于过期时间，断言该值此时已视为过期（或按现有约定的边界语义，明确写出并断言）。
- 同时断言到期前一刻（过期时间减 1ms 或最小单位）值仍然有效。
- 整个测试文件全部通过，报告里附测试输出。

不要做的事：不要改动已有测试的语义，不要改 values() 或其它功能代码，不要合并、推送或开 PR。

工作地点：~/dev/foo，分支 ducter/foo-ttl，在该分支上提交。
```

```
我们 bridge 不知道是否有唤醒能力，如果没有，暂时需要你自己去 loop 唤醒自己读取 bridge 信箱

不过我这边没法把它固定成 Codex，每件交过来的活都会交给适合的 agent 去做。要的话，我可以再存一条规则：代码类的活优先用 Codex。
```
